## Runtime work elimination — 2026-09-08

[Raw trials, controls, profiles, and helpers](2026-09-08-runtime.txt). Issue #1.

The baseline is the scratch-refactor package described in 2026-09-08-scratch.txt.
Retained production code ends at `a4871df`. Reverts through `135b510` restore that source exactly.
Both z53 packages use ReleaseSafe and the baseline CPU target. Dependency pins remain unchanged.

### Retained changes

| Change | Source | Paired primary gain |
|---|---|---:|
| Enumerate configured health candidates instead of all 1024 slots | `9e37d3a` | 56.52% |
| Retain the synchronous logger buffer | `9ff8b6c` | 17.00% |
| Index stable cache slots and bound tombstone debt | `42a760f` + `a4871df` | 10.55% |

These percentages come from separate paired runs. The final comparison below measures their combined effect.
The health list preserves sparse endpoint identities and probe policy.
The logger exposes only the current line. Its synchronous sink can still block the event thread.
The cache keeps full-key collision checks, stable slots, and its existing packet ownership rules.

### Final paired comparison

The fixture uses 4096 warm names. It has 32 clients and permits at most 32 outstanding queries.
Each median covers three three-second trials. Resolver order rotates between trials.
Resolver CPU 2, generator CPUs 0/3, and upstream CPU 1 remain unchanged.
The host retains normal workloads. No CPU isolation or frequency control applies.

The primary case enables health and configures one owned upstream.
Completion logs remain enabled, with output to `/dev/null`.
The fixture excludes hosts and TLS. It does not persist service logs.

| Resolver | UDP QPS | TCP QPS | UDP CPU µs/query | TCP CPU µs/query |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Final scratch package | 93018 | 71873 | 9.262 | 11.926 |
| Retained runtime package | 191142 | 143936 | 4.253 | 5.244 |
| CoreDNS 1.14.6 | 89361 | 108664 | 9.776 | 7.177 |

The runtime package delivers 2.03 times the scratch package's primary geometric mean.
UDP throughput reaches 2.05 times the baseline. TCP throughput reaches 2.00 times the baseline.
z53 delivers 2.14 times CoreDNS UDP throughput and 1.32 times CoreDNS TCP throughput.
These are observed fixture results, not maximum sustainable capacity or production latency.

| Secondary case | Scratch UDP/TCP QPS | Runtime UDP/TCP QPS |
|---|---:|---:|
| Health disabled, one upstream | 96639 / 72781 | 194280 / 143665 |
| Health enabled, sixteen upstream members | 94930 / 71786 | 187872 / 139235 |

All 42 final timed trials complete 15358431 queries, with zero losses and only NOERROR responses.
No timed trial increases the owned upstream log. No query reaches production or a public upstream.
Median sampled RSS rises from 60436 KiB to 60600 KiB between the z53 packages. CoreDNS records 60216 KiB.
These resident measurements do not replace allocated storage bounds.

### Cache maintenance and memory

The first index improved warm hits but accumulated tombstones until no free bucket remained.
The longer aged fixture performs 320000 replacements before 10000 timed operations.
Each result below is the median of three trials. All report zero bytes and allocations per operation.

| Index | Miss, ns/op | Insert/evict, ns/op |
|---|---:|---:|
| Previous linear scan | 2659 | 5359 |
| Index without maintenance | 33905 | 71832 |
| Index with bounded removal debt | 36.09 | 164.1 |

The timed churn interval includes periodic rehashes. These index-only results exclude DNS and packet-pool work.
Separate clock-instrumented trials record p99 values of 240–243 ns and three rehashes per 10000 operations.
Rehash maxima range from 116906 to 119126 ns. The occasional 119 µs stall is a real tail cost.
Collision-dependent probes prevent a strict linear worst-case claim. These measurements do not establish DNS response percentiles.

An enabled cache now makes five startup allocations instead of three.
Default metadata rises from 6320000 to 6483888 bytes across both banks.
The allocated metadata bound rises from 320 to 384 bytes per configured entry, with index headers included.
The `Entry` size limit remains 320 bytes. Packet budgets and fixed runtime caps remain unchanged.

### Rejected candidates and warmup

Three later candidates miss the five-percent throughput gate:

- Question-name output reuse: 0.54%.
- UTC prefix cache: 5.13% initially, then 3.11% in an independent confirmation.
- Compile-time RFC 6761 names: 2.24%.

Targeted reverts remove all three changes. The campaign stops at this measured plateau.

Three earlier comparisons stop during untimed dnsperf warmup. Their partial captures remain in the evidence.
One CoreDNS-only trial takes 4.344 seconds for 4096 replies, with 28 µs average DNS latency and zero loss.
The exact timeout mechanism remains unknown.

Final warmup uses a sequential UDP client with the same names and one outstanding request.
It permits no retry and applies a one-second deadline to each exchange.
Eight controls reject incorrect responses or a timeout. A disabled flag check makes the control suite fail.
Timed dnsperf commands remain unchanged. Every timed trial still requires zero loss and only NOERROR replies.
The owned upstream log must remain unchanged during each timed trial.
The raw capture embeds both warmup implementations and their controls.

### Checks and limits

The final source passes these native ReleaseSafe checks:

- 42 wire tests and 62 resolver tests.
- 60 forwarding tests.
- 21 health tests.
- 13 logger tests.
- 16 configuration tests and four foundation tests.
- The benchmark smoke.
- Source style and whitespace checks.

The executable and all test roots pass semantic compilation for x86_64 Linux and aarch64 macOS.
Those checks do not establish native execution on either target.
Negative controls cover sparse endpoint IDs and retained logger bytes.
Cache controls reject fingerprint-only matches and missing index retirement.
A reserve test fails before tombstone maintenance, then passes with the fix.

The final userspace profile contains 582 samples, with none lost.
Name decode accounts for 8.49% of samples. Timestamp formatting accounts for 6.84%, and packet parsing accounts for 4.46%.
The remaining costs span several functions. Unresolved samples account for 14.57%.
Sample shares do not measure request-latency attribution.


Capture notes

Personal planning notes are omitted. Measured data and retained artifact hashes are unchanged.

The capture indents diagnostic separators to avoid Git conflict-marker checks. It removes trailing whitespace. Original local artifacts remain unchanged. Hashes identify the original artifacts.

--- .lab/runtime/compare-dnsperf-warm.py ---
SHA256 f009f697fdee3f72e95cd244293c7ee88cef1cacbf055322713094ff71dece82

import argparse
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import random
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import time

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('bench', 'scripts/compare-coredns.py')
bench = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(bench)
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument('--variant', action='append', required=True, help='label=binary')
parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
parser.add_argument('--trials', type=int, default=3)
parser.add_argument('--duration', type=int, default=3)
parser.add_argument('--cases', default='2:1,0:1,2:16')
parser.add_argument('--profile', action='store_true')
args = parser.parse_args()
assert 1 <= args.trials <= 5 and 1 <= args.duration <= 10
cases = [tuple(map(int, case.split(':'))) for case in args.cases.split(',')]
assert all(health in (0, 2) and 1 <= members <= 16 for health, members in cases)
lock = open('.lab/measurement.lock', 'w')
fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
root = args.output
root.mkdir()
variants = [(label, str(Path(binary).resolve())) for label, binary in (v.split('=', 1) for v in args.variant)]
metadata = dict(source=bench.command(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD']).strip(), optimization='ReleaseSafe',
                target='baseline unless explicitly labeled', cases=cases, trials=args.trials,
                duration_s=args.duration, names=4096, resolver_cpu=2, generator_cpus=[0,3], upstream_cpu=1,
                logging='/dev/null', GOMAXPROCS=1, uname=list(os.uname()),
                binaries={label:dict(path=binary, sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(binary).read_bytes()).hexdigest())
                          for label,binary in variants})
(root/'metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2)+'\n')
names = [f'q{index:05}.bench.test. A\n' for index in range(4096)]
random.Random(53).shuffle(names)
data = root/'queries.txt'
data.write_text(''.join(names))
upstream = bench.port()
upstream_log = root/'upstream.log'
config = root/'upstream.Corefile'
config.write_text(f'''bench.test:{upstream} {{
 bind 127.0.0.1
 log
 template IN A {{
  answer "{{{{ .Name }}}} 3600 IN A 192.0.2.1"
 }}
}}
''')
rows=[]
with bench.server(shutil.which('coredns'), config, upstream, upstream_log, 1):
    for health,members in cases:
        for trial in range(args.trials):
            start = trial % len(variants)
            for label,binary in variants[start:]+variants[:start]:
                number = bench.port()
                prefix = f'h{health}-m{members}-t{trial}-{label}'
                config = root/(prefix+'.conf')
                if Path(binary).name == 'coredns':
                    peers = ' '.join([f'127.0.0.1:{upstream}']*members)
                    config.write_text(f'.:{number} {{\n bind 127.0.0.1\n log\n errors\n cache 3600\n forward . {peers} {{\n policy sequential\n max_fails {health}\n }}\n}}\n')
                else:
                    peers = ','.join([f'.{{ .address = "127.0.0.1:{upstream}" }}']*members)
                    config.write_text(f'.{{ .listen = .{{ "127.0.0.1:{number}" }}, .zones = .{{ .{{ .suffix = ".", .upstreams = .{{ {peers} }}, .max_fails = {health} }} }} }}')
                with bench.server(binary, config, number, os.devnull, 2) as process:
                    try:
                        warm = bench.measure(root,data,number,'udp',1,prefix+'-warm')
                    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as error:
                        detail = dict(command=error.cmd, timeout_s=error.timeout,
                                      resolver_pid=process.pid, resolver_status=process.poll())
                        (root/(prefix+'-warm-timeout.json')).write_text(json.dumps(detail,indent=2)+'\n')
                        for stream,value in [('stdout',error.stdout),('stderr',error.stderr)]:
                            if value is not None:
                                text=value.decode(errors='replace') if isinstance(value,bytes) else value
                                (root/(prefix+'-warm-'+stream+'.txt')).write_text(text)
                        raise
                    assert warm['completed']==4096 and warm['lost']==0
                    upstream_bytes = upstream_log.stat().st_size
                    for protocol in ['udp','tcp']:
                        case = prefix+'-'+protocol
                        recorder = None
                        if args.profile and protocol=='udp':
                            command = ['perf','record','-F','199','-e','cycles:u','--call-graph','dwarf',
                                       '-p',str(process.pid),'-o',str(root/(case+'.data')),'--','sleep',str(args.duration+1)]
                            recorder = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
                            time.sleep(.1)
                        before = bench.cpu_seconds(process.pid)
                        try:
                            result = bench.measure(root,data,number,protocol,32,case,args.duration)
                        finally:
                            if recorder is not None:
                                output,_ = recorder.communicate(timeout=args.duration+3)
                                (root/(case+'-record.txt')).write_text(' '.join(command)+'\n'+output)
                                assert recorder.returncode==0,output
                        result.update(variant=label,health=health,members=members,trial=trial,protocol=protocol,
                                      cpu_s=bench.cpu_seconds(process.pid)-before,rss_kib=bench.rss_kib(process.pid),
                                      upstream_bytes=upstream_log.stat().st_size-upstream_bytes)
                        assert result['sent']==result['completed'] and result['lost']==0
                        assert result['upstream_bytes']==0
                        rows.append(result)
                        (root/'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+'\n')
                        print(json.dumps(result),flush=True)
                        if recorder is not None:
                            report=bench.command(['perf','report','--stdio','--no-children','--call-graph','none','--percent-limit','.5','-i',str(root/(case+'.data'))])
                            (root/(case+'-report.txt')).write_text(report)
summary=[]
for health,members in cases:
    for label,_ in variants:
        qps=[]
        for protocol in ['udp','tcp']:
            group=[row for row in rows if row['health']==health and row['members']==members and row['variant']==label and row['protocol']==protocol]
            values=[row['qps'] for row in group]
            qps.append(statistics.median(values))
            summary.append(dict(health=health,members=members,variant=label,protocol=protocol,median=qps[-1],minimum=min(values),maximum=max(values),
                                cpu_us=statistics.median(row['cpu_s']/row['completed']*1e6 for row in group)))
        print(label,health,members,'primary',math.sqrt(math.prod(qps)))
(root/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n')


--- .lab/runtime/compare.py ---
SHA256 377176246030f4acb38e0b9765fabd76e773d9bac35333f91a06491a9914519b

import argparse
import fcntl
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import random
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import time
from warm import warm as warm_queries

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('bench', 'scripts/compare-coredns.py')
bench = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(bench)
parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument('--variant', action='append', required=True, help='label=binary')
parser.add_argument('--output', type=Path, required=True)
parser.add_argument('--trials', type=int, default=3)
parser.add_argument('--duration', type=int, default=3)
parser.add_argument('--cases', default='2:1,0:1,2:16')
parser.add_argument('--profile', action='store_true')
args = parser.parse_args()
assert 1 <= args.trials <= 5 and 1 <= args.duration <= 10
cases = [tuple(map(int, case.split(':'))) for case in args.cases.split(',')]
assert all(health in (0, 2) and 1 <= members <= 16 for health, members in cases)
lock = open('.lab/measurement.lock', 'w')
fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
root = args.output
root.mkdir()
variants = [(label, str(Path(binary).resolve())) for label, binary in (v.split('=', 1) for v in args.variant)]
metadata = dict(source=bench.command(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD']).strip(), optimization='ReleaseSafe',
                target='baseline unless explicitly labeled', cases=cases, trials=args.trials,
                duration_s=args.duration, names=4096, resolver_cpu=2, generator_cpus=[0,3], upstream_cpu=1,
                logging='/dev/null', GOMAXPROCS=1, uname=list(os.uname()),
                warmup='sequential UDP, one outstanding, one-second deadline, no retries',
                binaries={label:dict(path=binary, sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(binary).read_bytes()).hexdigest())
                          for label,binary in variants})
(root/'metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata, indent=2)+'\n')
names = [f'q{index:05}.bench.test. A\n' for index in range(4096)]
random.Random(53).shuffle(names)
data = root/'queries.txt'
data.write_text(''.join(names))
upstream = bench.port()
upstream_log = root/'upstream.log'
config = root/'upstream.Corefile'
config.write_text(f'''bench.test:{upstream} {{
 bind 127.0.0.1
 log
 template IN A {{
  answer "{{{{ .Name }}}} 3600 IN A 192.0.2.1"
 }}
}}
''')
rows=[]
with bench.server(shutil.which('coredns'), config, upstream, upstream_log, 1):
    for health,members in cases:
        for trial in range(args.trials):
            start = trial % len(variants)
            for label,binary in variants[start:]+variants[:start]:
                number = bench.port()
                prefix = f'h{health}-m{members}-t{trial}-{label}'
                config = root/(prefix+'.conf')
                if Path(binary).name == 'coredns':
                    peers = ' '.join([f'127.0.0.1:{upstream}']*members)
                    config.write_text(f'.:{number} {{\n bind 127.0.0.1\n log\n errors\n cache 3600\n forward . {peers} {{\n policy sequential\n max_fails {health}\n }}\n}}\n')
                else:
                    peers = ','.join([f'.{{ .address = "127.0.0.1:{upstream}" }}']*members)
                    config.write_text(f'.{{ .listen = .{{ "127.0.0.1:{number}" }}, .zones = .{{ .{{ .suffix = ".", .upstreams = .{{ {peers} }}, .max_fails = {health} }} }} }}')
                with bench.server(binary, config, number, os.devnull, 2) as process:
                    try:
                        warm = warm_queries(root,data,number,prefix+'-warm')
                    except (subprocess.TimeoutExpired, TimeoutError) as error:
                        detail = dict(command=getattr(error,'cmd',None), timeout_s=getattr(error,'timeout',1),
                                      resolver_pid=process.pid, resolver_status=process.poll())
                        (root/(prefix+'-warm-timeout.json')).write_text(json.dumps(detail,indent=2)+'\n')
                        for stream,value in [('stdout',getattr(error,'stdout',None)),('stderr',getattr(error,'stderr',None))]:
                            if value is not None:
                                text=value.decode(errors='replace') if isinstance(value,bytes) else value
                                (root/(prefix+'-warm-'+stream+'.txt')).write_text(text)
                        raise
                    assert warm['sent']==4096 and warm['completed']==4096 and warm['lost']==0
                    upstream_bytes = upstream_log.stat().st_size
                    for protocol in ['udp','tcp']:
                        case = prefix+'-'+protocol
                        recorder = None
                        if args.profile and protocol=='udp':
                            command = ['perf','record','-F','199','-e','cycles:u','--call-graph','dwarf',
                                       '-p',str(process.pid),'-o',str(root/(case+'.data')),'--','sleep',str(args.duration+1)]
                            recorder = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
                            time.sleep(.1)
                        before = bench.cpu_seconds(process.pid)
                        try:
                            result = bench.measure(root,data,number,protocol,32,case,args.duration)
                        finally:
                            if recorder is not None:
                                output,_ = recorder.communicate(timeout=args.duration+3)
                                (root/(case+'-record.txt')).write_text(' '.join(command)+'\n'+output)
                                assert recorder.returncode==0,output
                        result.update(variant=label,health=health,members=members,trial=trial,protocol=protocol,
                                      cpu_s=bench.cpu_seconds(process.pid)-before,rss_kib=bench.rss_kib(process.pid),
                                      upstream_bytes=upstream_log.stat().st_size-upstream_bytes)
                        assert result['sent']==result['completed'] and result['lost']==0
                        assert result['upstream_bytes']==0
                        rows.append(result)
                        (root/'results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+'\n')
                        print(json.dumps(result),flush=True)
                        if recorder is not None:
                            report=bench.command(['perf','report','--stdio','--no-children','--call-graph','none','--percent-limit','.5','-i',str(root/(case+'.data'))])
                            (root/(case+'-report.txt')).write_text(report)
summary=[]
for health,members in cases:
    for label,_ in variants:
        qps=[]
        for protocol in ['udp','tcp']:
            group=[row for row in rows if row['health']==health and row['members']==members and row['variant']==label and row['protocol']==protocol]
            values=[row['qps'] for row in group]
            qps.append(statistics.median(values))
            summary.append(dict(health=health,members=members,variant=label,protocol=protocol,median=qps[-1],minimum=min(values),maximum=max(values),
                                cpu_us=statistics.median(row['cpu_s']/row['completed']*1e6 for row in group)))
        print(label,health,members,'primary',math.sqrt(math.prod(qps)))
(root/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n')


--- .lab/runtime/warm.py ---
SHA256 4a3f031a0e86170e5f40754707031894808375614160a8c82c0c41fd34964733

"""Untimed warmup with one UDP request outstanding and no retry."""
import json
import socket
import struct
import time


def warm(root, data, number, label):
    lines=data.read_text().splitlines()
    assert len(lines)==4096
    target=('127.0.0.1',number)
    started=time.monotonic_ns()
    duration_ns_max=0
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET,socket.SOCK_DGRAM) as client:
        client.settimeout(1)
        for identifier,line in enumerate(lines):
            name,kind=line.split()
            assert kind=='A' and name.endswith('.bench.test.')
            labels=name.rstrip('.').split('.')
            question=b''.join(bytes([len(label)])+label.encode('ascii') for label in labels)
            question+=b'\x00'+struct.pack('!HH',1,1)
            request=struct.pack('!HHHHHH',identifier,0x0100,1,0,0,0)+question
            before=time.monotonic_ns()
            assert client.sendto(request,target)==len(request)
            try:
                response,peer=client.recvfrom(65535)
            except TimeoutError:
                (root/(label+'-timeout.json')).write_text(json.dumps(dict(identifier=identifier,name=name,completed=identifier),indent=2)+'\n')
                raise
            duration_ns_max=max(duration_ns_max,time.monotonic_ns()-before)
            assert peer==target and len(response)>=12
            returned,flags,questions,answers,authority,additional=struct.unpack('!HHHHHH',response[:12])
            assert returned==identifier and flags&0x820f==0x8000
            assert questions==1 and answers==1 and authority==0 and additional==0
            assert response[12:12+len(question)]==question
    result=dict(sent=len(lines),completed=len(lines),lost=0,rcode='NOERROR',
                elapsed_ns=time.monotonic_ns()-started,duration_ns_max=duration_ns_max,
                method='sequential UDP, one outstanding, one-second deadline, no retries')
    (root/(label+'.json')).write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    return result


--- .lab/runtime/warm-controls.py ---
SHA256 ed5acb7801a5a2367564aabe31ea4a67ea019f692a86d2c26944fbd656264d80

import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import struct
import tempfile
from unittest.mock import patch
import warm


class Peer:
    mode='valid'

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self,*args):
        return False

    def settimeout(self,value):
        assert value==1

    def sendto(self,request,target):
        self.request=request
        self.target=target
        return len(request)

    def recvfrom(self,maximum):
        assert maximum==65535
        if self.mode=='timeout':
            raise TimeoutError()
        if self.mode=='short':
            return b'X',self.target
        identifier=struct.unpack('!H',self.request[:2])[0]
        flags=0x8180
        questions=1
        if self.mode=='id':
            identifier^=1
        if self.mode=='rcode':
            flags|=3
        if self.mode=='tc':
            flags|=0x200
        if self.mode=='qr':
            flags&=0x7fff
        if self.mode=='count':
            questions=0
        question=self.request[12:]
        if self.mode=='question':
            question=b'X'+question[1:]
        header=struct.pack('!HHHHHH',identifier,flags,questions,1,0,0)
        answer=b'\xc0\x0c'+struct.pack('!HHIH',1,1,3600,4)+bytes([192,0,2,1])
        return header+question+answer,self.target


with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='z53-warm-control-') as directory:
    root=Path(directory)
    data=root/'queries.txt'
    data.write_text(''.join(f'q{x:05}.bench.test. A\n' for x in range(4096)))
    for mode in ['valid','id','rcode','tc','qr','count','question','short','timeout']:
        Peer.mode=mode
        with patch.object(socket,'socket',return_value=Peer()):
            try:
                result=warm.warm(root,data,12345,mode)
            except (AssertionError,TimeoutError):
                assert mode!='valid'
                print('rejected',mode)
            else:
                assert mode=='valid',mode
                assert result['sent']==result['completed']==4096 and result['lost']==0
                print('accepted',mode,result['completed'])
print('WARM_CONTROLS_PASS')


--- .lab/runtime/index-churn.zig ---
SHA256 c02ee48b7759028c69fbd5a9c9ba7335ae89b31de758db07cf498b17b5eb161a

//! SPEC §§1.10, 3.7, 9.4: aged index costs, not DNS throughput or packet-pool costs.
const std = @import("std");
const benchmark = @import("benchmark");
const cache = @import("cache");
const Bank = @TypeOf(@as(cache.Cache, undefined).positive);
const Key = @TypeOf(@as(cache.Entry, undefined).key);
const capacity = 10000;
const aged_insertions = 32 * capacity;
const iterations = 10000;
const Mode = enum { miss, churn };

pub fn main(init: std.process.Init) !void {
    const cases = [_]benchmark.Spec{
        .{ .name = "AgedIndexMiss10000", .func = Runner(.miss).run },
        .{ .name = "AgedIndexChurn10000", .func = Runner(.churn).run },
    };
    if (!try benchmark.runBenchmarks(init.gpa, &cases, .{
        .io = init.io,
        .benchtime = .{ .count = iterations },
        .count = 3,
        .benchmem = true,
    })) return error.BenchmarkFailed;
}

fn keyInit(key: *Key, index: u32) !void {
    var text: [48]u8 = undefined;
    try key.name.fromText(try std.fmt.bufPrint(&text, "q{d}.aged.example.", .{index}));
    key.kind = 1;
    key.class = 1;
    key.dnssec = .ordinary;
}

fn prepare(bank: *Bank, payload: []u8, inputs: []Key) !void {
    var key: Key = undefined;
    for (0..capacity) |index| {
        try keyInit(&key, @intCast(index));
        bank.put(@intCast(index), &.{ .key = key, .bytes = payload[index..][0..1] });
    }
    for (0..aged_insertions) |index| {
        const slot: u32 = @intCast(index % capacity);
        const owned = bank.take(slot);
        try keyInit(&key, capacity + @as(u32, @intCast(index)));
        bank.put(slot, &.{ .key = key, .bytes = owned });
    }
    for (inputs, 0..) |*input, index| {
        try keyInit(input, capacity + aged_insertions + @as(u32, @intCast(index)));
    }
}

fn Runner(comptime mode: Mode) type {
    return struct {
        fn run(timer: *benchmark.B) !void {
            var bank: Bank = .{};
            try bank.init(timer.allocator, capacity);
            defer bank.deinit(timer.allocator);
            // Distinct one-byte slices model stable payload ownership without a packet allocator.
            const payload = try timer.allocator.alloc(u8, capacity);
            defer timer.allocator.free(payload);
            const inputs = try timer.allocator.alloc(Key, iterations);
            defer timer.allocator.free(inputs);
            try prepare(&bank, payload, inputs);
            var free: u32 = 0;
            if (@hasField(Bank, "index")) {
                for (bank.index.metadata.?[0..bank.index.capacity()]) |metadata| {
                    if (metadata.isFree()) free += 1;
                }
            }
            var index: u32 = 0;
            while (try timer.loop()) : (index += 1) {
                const key = benchmark.blackBox(&inputs[index]);
                if (mode == .miss) {
                    if (bank.find(key) != null) return error.UnexpectedHit;
                } else {
                    const slot = bank.slot(key);
                    const owned = bank.take(slot);
                    bank.put(slot, &.{ .key = key.*, .bytes = owned });
                }
                benchmark.keepAlive(&bank);
            }
            if (@hasField(Bank, "index")) try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(free), "free-buckets-before");
        }
    };
}


--- .lab/runtime/index-churn-tails.zig ---
SHA256 872dc0a86cbeeb77b7f4f4a60fab81942726cc52431d5b972e48b808f46f82f6

//! SPEC §§1.10, 3.7, 9.4: aged index costs, not DNS throughput or packet-pool costs.
const std = @import("std");
const benchmark = @import("benchmark");
const cache = @import("cache");
const Bank = @TypeOf(@as(cache.Cache, undefined).positive);
const Key = @TypeOf(@as(cache.Entry, undefined).key);
const capacity = 10000;
const aged_insertions = 32 * capacity;
const iterations = 10000;
const Mode = enum { miss, churn };

pub fn main(init: std.process.Init) !void {
    const cases = [_]benchmark.Spec{
        .{ .name = "AgedIndexMiss10000", .func = Runner(.miss).run },
        .{ .name = "AgedIndexChurn10000", .func = Runner(.churn).run },
    };
    if (!try benchmark.runBenchmarks(init.gpa, &cases, .{
        .io = init.io,
        .benchtime = .{ .count = iterations },
        .count = 3,
        .benchmem = true,
    })) return error.BenchmarkFailed;
}

fn keyInit(key: *Key, index: u32) !void {
    var text: [48]u8 = undefined;
    try key.name.fromText(try std.fmt.bufPrint(&text, "q{d}.aged.example.", .{index}));
    key.kind = 1;
    key.class = 1;
    key.dnssec = .ordinary;
}

fn prepare(bank: *Bank, payload: []u8, inputs: []Key) !void {
    var key: Key = undefined;
    for (0..capacity) |index| {
        try keyInit(&key, @intCast(index));
        bank.put(@intCast(index), &.{ .key = key, .bytes = payload[index..][0..1] });
    }
    for (0..aged_insertions) |index| {
        const slot: u32 = @intCast(index % capacity);
        const owned = bank.take(slot);
        try keyInit(&key, capacity + @as(u32, @intCast(index)));
        bank.put(slot, &.{ .key = key, .bytes = owned });
    }
    for (inputs, 0..) |*input, index| {
        try keyInit(input, capacity + aged_insertions + @as(u32, @intCast(index)));
    }
}

fn Runner(comptime mode: Mode) type {
    return struct {
        fn run(timer: *benchmark.B) !void {
            var bank: Bank = .{};
            try bank.init(timer.allocator, capacity);
            defer bank.deinit(timer.allocator);
            // Distinct one-byte slices model stable payload ownership without a packet allocator.
            const payload = try timer.allocator.alloc(u8, capacity);
            defer timer.allocator.free(payload);
            const inputs = try timer.allocator.alloc(Key, iterations);
            defer timer.allocator.free(inputs);
            try prepare(&bank, payload, inputs);
            const times = try timer.allocator.alloc(u64, iterations);
            defer timer.allocator.free(times);
            var rehashes: u32 = 0;
            var rehash_ns_max: u64 = 0;
            var free: u32 = 0;
            if (@hasField(Bank, "index")) {
                for (bank.index.metadata.?[0..bank.index.capacity()]) |metadata| {
                    if (metadata.isFree()) free += 1;
                }
            }
            var index: u32 = 0;
            while (try timer.loop()) : (index += 1) {
                const key = benchmark.blackBox(&inputs[index]);
                const started = if (mode == .churn) timer.elapsed() else 0;
                if (mode == .miss) {
                    if (bank.find(key) != null) return error.UnexpectedHit;
                } else {
                    const slot = bank.slot(key);
                    const owned = bank.take(slot);
                    bank.put(slot, &.{ .key = key.*, .bytes = owned });
                    times[index] = timer.elapsed() - started;
                    if (@hasField(Bank, "index_removals")) {
                        if (bank.index_removals == 0) {
                            rehashes += 1;
                            rehash_ns_max = @max(rehash_ns_max, times[index]);
                        }
                    }
                }
                benchmark.keepAlive(&bank);
            }
            if (@hasField(Bank, "index")) try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(free), "free-buckets-before");
            if (mode == .churn) {
                std.sort.heap(u64, times, {}, std.sort.asc(u64));
                try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(times[iterations * 99 / 100]), "p99-ns");
                try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(times[iterations - 1]), "max-ns");
                try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(rehashes), "rehashes");
                try timer.reportMetric(@floatFromInt(rehash_ns_max), "rehash-max-ns");
            }
        }
    };
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
benchmark=zig-pkg/benchmark-0.1.0-JS72VkomAQAHiEPwJB4rOn0vjLChZnQhcMqVKLPFZCQ4/src
zig build-exe -OReleaseSafe -mcpu=baseline --dep cache --dep benchmark \
  -Mroot=.lab/runtime/index-churn.zig \
  -OReleaseSafe -mcpu=baseline -Mcache="$1/cache.zig" \
  -OReleaseSafe -mcpu=baseline --dep benchmark_filter -Mbenchmark="$benchmark/benchmark.zig" \
  -OReleaseSafe -mcpu=baseline -Mbenchmark_filter="$benchmark/filter.zig" \
  -lc -femit-bin="$2"
